With --reaudit, checks a fix round against its own diff; with --consolidate-hotfixes, reclassifies hotfix trace docs against the current code. ([pe-menezes/vibeflow](https://github.com/pe-menezes/vibeflow/blob/HEAD/opencode/commands/vibeflow-audit.md) · ★34)

- **view.hu**: Így fordít az AutoCAD NÉZET (VIEW, N, -NÉZET) parancsa a hat ortografikus és a négy izometrikus nézetre, és milyen FKR-t állít be az ortografikus. Próbálja ki! ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/view.hu.md))

- **view.pl**: Jak w AutoCADzie obrócić widok poleceniem WIDOK (VIEW, -WIDOK): sześć widoków prostokątnych i cztery izometryczne oraz LUW ustawiany przez każdy prostokątny. ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/view.pl.md))

- **vision-adjust.template**: This prompt was authored for Claude-style slash workflows. In Codex runtime, adapt tool calls as follows: - `AskUserQuestion` =&gt; ask the user directly in chat. - `WebSearch`/`WebFetch` =&gt; use available web tools (`search_query`, `open`, `find`) and cite links. - `Read`/`Write` =&gt; use shell/filesystem tools in this workspace. - Claude-only MCP calls (for example Playwright MCP names) =&gt; use availab ([evan043/claude-cli-advanced-starter-pack](https://github.com/evan043/claude-cli-advanced-starter-pack/blob/HEAD/templates/commands/vision-adjust.template.md) · ★62)
- **vision-board-build.contract**: Use `--repo &lt;path&gt;` for a Git target or `--workspace &lt;path&gt;` for a new-idea workspace. Both inputs are explicit readable files, resolved from the caller's directory. The draft is nonempty UTF-8 Markdown. Proposals conform to `skills/vision/schemas/vision-proposals.json`: ([knowttl/bstack](https://github.com/knowttl/bstack/blob/HEAD/runtime/scripts/commands/vision-board-build.contract.md))
- **vision-board-launch.contract**: Both commands also accept --repo for a Git target. Resume uses the explicit board path from an earlier build, preserving its original draft and proposals. The manifest target, original exact-byte draft hash and ordered proposal IDs must still agree. A different target or changed draft fails with incompatible-board before launch or new scratch creation. Missing board files or inaccessible resources ([knowttl/bstack](https://github.com/knowttl/bstack/blob/HEAD/runtime/scripts/commands/vision-board-launch.contract.md))
- **vision-board-verdicts.contract**: Verdicts takes the complete-round Context data JSON embedded in the terminal's vision-verdict prompt. Copy that JSON unchanged to a scratch file, retaining the captured terminal response as provenance. The [vision-verdicts schema source](../../schemas/vision-verdicts.json) defines the input fields and accepted verdict labels: ([knowttl/bstack](https://github.com/knowttl/bstack/blob/HEAD/runtime/scripts/commands/vision-board-verdicts.contract.md))
